# Graph Report - .  (2026-08-04)

## Corpus Check
- 7 files · ~372,030 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 11715 nodes · 13436 edges · 941 communities (880 shown, 61 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 357 edges (avg confidence: 0.77)
- Token cost: 6,665 input · 2,018 output
